## Layered construction patch V2–V4 (10 November 2026)

Previous goal turn made concrete progress through V21 UV correction, original/reference review and rejected sparse V1 sculpt. This turn follows Root's stop-full-roof directive with isolated construction geometry. Worktree <HOME>/Work/DinkHouseExteriorSourceAdmission, branch art/house439-shared-exterior-source. QA studies: <HOME>/Work/DinkQA/PhotorealSessions/House439Facade/FullCottageRoofSuccessorV1/LayeredThatchPatchV2, V3, V4. Full all-assets/game objective remains active.

V2 adds compact continuous backing and coherent reed inclination (20 degree target, capped 320mm internal drop), with shortened lower eave layers, correcting V1's effectively parallel sparse wires. Actual two views show meaningful layered mass but exposed lower backing still a blunt fascia. Fresh saved audit catches negative signed volume on BOTH new backing and reed aggregate despite closure. ORIENTATION_FAILURE.json preserves evidence; no adoption. V3 reverses complete face order with matching corner UV order, retaining all positions. Fresh saved closure and positive aggregate volumes pass; actual two views confirm same shape and remaining blunt edge.

V4 recesses/rounds the backing behind the exposed butts, adds two lower depth layers (seven including first course), and limits reed tail drop into the supporting coat instead of through the underside. Four roof courses retained; 1430 original closed reed stems,45760tri plus32tri compact bed and24tri timber supports. All four saved mesh objects closed, positive signed volumes; separate REED_COMPONENT_VOLUMES readback proves all1430 reed components positive (min4.77891236e-6m3), not merely aggregate. REAR_SUPPORT_READBACK tests all7968 rear-ring vertices within explicit finite study x/s interior: all inside compact backing by first outward coat-normal exit, zero failures.612 vertices at finite study x/s boundaries separately recorded, not silently counted as passes.
